*Guojing Li,Zichuan Fu,Junyi Li,Faxue Liu,Wenxia Zhou,Yejing Wang,Jingtong Gao,Maolin Wang,Rungen Liu,Wenlin Zhang,Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Span-level skill extraction from job advertisements underpins candidate-job matching and labor-market analytics, yet generative large language models (LLMs) often yield malformed spans, boundary drift, and hallucinations, especially with long-tail terms and cross-domain shift. We present SRICL, an LLM-centric framework that combines semantic retrieval (SR), in-context learning (ICL), and supervised fine-tuning (SFT) with a deterministic verifier. SR pulls in-domain annotated sentences and definitions from ESCO to form format-constrained prompts that stabilize boundaries and handle coordination. SFT aligns output behavior, while the verifier enforces pairing, non-overlap, and BIO legality with minimal retries. On six public span-labeled corpora of job-ad sentences across sectors and languages, SRICL achieves substantial STRICT-F1 improvements over GPT-3.5 prompting baselines and sharply reduces invalid tags and hallucinated spans, enabling dependable sentence-level deployment in low-resource, multi-domain settings.

</details>


### [39] [UKP_Psycontrol at SemEval-2026 Task 2: Modeling Valence and Arousal Dynamics from Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21534)
*Darya Hryhoryeva,Amaia Zurinaga,Hamidreza Jamalabadi,Iryna Gurevych*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: A multimodal approach combining LLM prompting, MaxEnt structured modeling, and neural regression for affective analysis in user-generated texts, achieving top ranks in SemEval-2026 Task 2.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SemEval-2026 Task 2 requires modeling both current affect and short-term affective change in chronologically ordered user-generated texts, presenting a dual challenge of static sentiment detection and dynamic affective transition tracking.

Method: Three complementary approaches: (1) LLM prompting with user-aware/user-agnostic settings; (2) Pairwise Maximum Entropy model with Ising-style interactions for structured transition modeling; (3) Lightweight neural regression model incorporating recent affective trajectories and trainable user embeddings.

Result: LLMs effectively capture static affective signals from text, while short-term affective variation is more strongly explained by recent numeric state trajectories than by textual semantics, suggesting that temporal dynamics play a crucial role in affective prediction.

Conclusion: The proposed system achieved first place in both Subtask 1 and Subtask 2A among all participating teams, demonstrating the effectiveness of combining multiple complementary approaches for affective modeling.

Abstract: This paper presents our system developed for SemEval-2026 Task 2. The task requires modeling both current affect and short-term affective change in chronologically ordered user-generated texts. We explore three complementary approaches: (1) LLM prompting under user-aware and user-agnostic settings, (2) a pairwise Maximum Entropy (MaxEnt) model with Ising-style interactions for structured transition modeling, and (3) a lightweight neural regression model incorporating recent affective trajectories and trainable user embeddings. Our findings indicate that LLMs effectively capture static affective signals from text, whereas short-term affective variation in this dataset is more strongly explained by recent numeric state trajectories than by textual semantics. Our system ranked first among participating teams in both Subtask 1 and Subtask 2A based on the official evaluation metric.

</details>


### [40] [Finding Meaning in Embeddings: Concept Separation Curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21555)
*Paul Keuren,Marc Ponsen,Robert Ayoub Bagheri*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需分类器的句子嵌入评估方法——概念分离曲线（Concept Separation Curves），通过引入句法噪声和语义否定来量化嵌入对概念层面变化的敏感性，从而实现对句子嵌入概念稳定性的可解释、可复现的跨模型评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有句子嵌入质量评估大多依赖额外分类器或下游任务，导致难以区分性能来源于嵌入本身还是分类器，存在评估结果不客观、解释性不足的问题。

Method: 提出分类器无关的评估框架，通过系统性引入句法噪声（syntactic noise）和语义否定（semantic negations），利用概念分离曲线可视化嵌入对概念变化的敏感程度，跨越荷兰语和英语两种语言、多个领域和不同句子长度进行实验。

Result: 概念分离曲线能够有效展示模型区分概念层面变化与表层变化的能力，实验结果表明该方法具有良好的可解释性、可复现性，并可跨模型进行对比。

Conclusion: Concept Separation Curves为评估句子嵌入的概念稳定性提供了一种客观、可解释且通用的方法，适用于多语言、多领域的嵌入质量评估。

Abstract: Sentence embedding techniques aim to encode key concepts of a sentence's meaning in a vector space. However, the majority of evaluation approaches for sentence embedding quality rely on the use of additional classifiers or downstream tasks. These additional components make it unclear whether good results stem from the embedding itself or from the classifier's behaviour. In this paper, we propose a novel method for evaluating the effectiveness of sentence embedding methods in capturing sentence-level concepts. Our approach is classifier-independent, allowing for an objective assessment of the model's performance. The approach adopted in this study involves the systematic introduction of syntactic noise and semantic negations into sentences, with the subsequent quantification of their relative effects on the resulting embeddings. The visualisation of these effects is facilitated by Concept Separation Curves, which show the model's capacity to differentiate between conceptual and surface-level variations. By leveraging data from multiple domains, employing both Dutch and English languages, and examining sentence lengths, this study offers a compelling demonstration that Concept Separation Curves provide an interpretable, reproducible, and cross-model approach for evaluating the conceptual stability of sentence embeddings.

</details>


### [41] [AgenticQwen: Training Small Agentic Language Models with Dual Data Flywheels for Industrial-Scale Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21590)
*Yuanjie Lyu,Chengyu Wang,Haonan Zheng,Yuanhao Yue,Junbing Yan,Ming Wang,Jun Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AgenticQwen是一系列小型智能体模型，通过多轮强化学习在合成数据和少量开源数据上训练，实现多步推理和工具调用能力，在多项智能体基准测试和工业系统中表现优异，缩小了与小模型的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代工业应用需要语言模型具备智能体能力（多步推理、工具使用），但需满足严格的成本和延迟约束，因此小型智能体模型需求迫切。

Method: 1) 多轮强化学习训练框架；2) 结合推理RL与智能体RL；3) 双向数据飞轮机制——推理飞轮通过错误学习增加任务难度，智能体飞轮将线性工作流扩展为多分支行为树；4) 使用合成数据与少量开源数据。

Result: 在多个智能体基准测试上表现强劲；在工业智能体系统中，搜索和数据分析任务接近更大模型的性能。

Conclusion: 通过多轮RL和双飞轮机制成功训练出高效的小型智能体模型，有效降低了工业智能体系统的部署成本和延迟。

Abstract: Modern industrial applications increasingly demand language models that act as agents, capable of multi-step reasoning and tool use in real-world settings. These tasks are typically performed under strict cost and latency constraints, making small agentic models highly desirable. In this paper, we introduce the AgenticQwen family of models, trained via multi-round reinforcement learning (RL) on synthetic data and a limited amount of open-source data. Our training framework combines reasoning RL and agentic RL with dual data flywheels that automatically generate increasingly challenging tasks. The reasoning flywheel increases task difficulty by learning from errors, while the agentic flywheel expands linear workflows into multi-branch behavior trees that better reflect the decision complexity of real-world applications. We validate AgenticQwen on public benchmarks and in an industrial agent system. The models achieve strong performance on multiple agentic benchmarks, and in our industrial agent system, close the gap with much larger models on search and data analysis tasks. Model checkpoints and part of the synthetic data: https://huggingface.co/collections/alibaba-pai/agenticqwen. Data synthesis and RL training code: https://github.com/haruhi-sudo/data_synth_and_rl. The data synthesis pipeline is also integrated into EasyDistill: https://github.com/modelscope/easydistill.

</details>


### [42] [Language as a Latent Variable for Reasoning Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21593)
*Linjuan Wu,Haoran Wei,Jialong Tang,Shuang Luo,Baosong Yang,Yongliang Shen,Weiming Lu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出polyGRPO框架，通过将语言变体作为隐式探索信号，利用多语言推理提升LLM的推理能力。核心发现是非英语响应有时优于英语，且语言无约束时性能最佳，表明多语言性扩展了模型的潜在推理空间。在仅使用18.1K多语言数学问题和无链式思维注释的情况下，Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct在英语推理测试集上提升6.72%，多语言基准上提升6.89%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观察到LLM减少英语中心偏见后，非英语响应在推理任务上出现超越英语的现象。传统观点认为语言仅为输出媒介，但研究者假设语言实际上作为隐变量结构性地调节模型的内部推理路径。

Method: 开展"多语言思维实验"(Polyglot Thinking Experiment)，在语言约束与无约束条件下测试模型；提出polyGRPO框架，将语言变体作为隐式探索信号，生成多语言偏好数据并同时优化答案准确性和推理结构。

Result: polyGRPO在仅训练18.1K多语言数学问题（无链式思维标注）的情况下：(1) 英语推理测试集绝对提升6.72%；(2) 多语言基准提升6.89%；(3) 是唯一在英语常识推理任务上超越基础LLM的方法（提升4.9%），展现了跨任务泛化能力。

Conclusion: 将语言作为隐变量处理能够扩展模型的潜在推理空间，带来一致且可泛化的推理性能提升。多语言性不仅是偏差来源，更是增强推理能力的有效手段。

Abstract: As LLMs reduce English-centric bias, a surprising trend emerges: non-English responses sometimes outperform English on reasoning tasks. We hypothesize that language functions as a latent variable that structurally modulates the model's internal inference pathways, rather than merely serving as an output medium. To test this, we conducted a Polyglot Thinking Experiment, in which models were prompted to solve identical problems under language-constrained and language-unconstrained conditions. Results show that non-English responses often achieve higher accuracy, and the best performance frequently occur when language is unconstrained, suggesting that multilinguality broadens the model's latent reasoning space. Based on this insight, we propose polyGRPO (Polyglot Group Relative Policy Optimization), an RL framework that treats language variation as an implicit exploration signal. It generates polyglot preference data online under language-constrained and unconstrained conditions, optimizing the policy with respect to both answer accuracy and reasoning structure. Trained on only 18.1K multilingual math problems without chain-of-thought annotations, polyGRPO improves the base model (Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct) by 6.72% absolute accuracy on four English reasoning testset and 6.89% in their multilingual benchmark. Remarkably, it is the only method that surpasses the base LLM on English commonsense reasoning task (4.9%), despite being trained solely on math data-highlighting its strong cross-task generalization. Further analysis reveals that treating language as a latent variable expands the model's latent reasoning space, yielding consistent and generalizable improvements in reasoning performance.

</details>


### [43] [Process Supervision via Verbal Critique Improves Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21611)
*Hao-Yuan Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Verbal Process Supervision (VPS)，利用强监督者的结构化自然语言批评来指导迭代式生成-批评-优化循环，作为推理时扩展的第四个维度（外部语言监督粒度）。实验表明 VPS 在 GPQA Diamond、AIME 2025 和 LiveCodeBench V6 上均取得显著提升，弱演员通过强监督者救援可达 63.3-90.0%，性能与监督者-执行者能力差距呈强正相关（Pearson r=0.90）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前推理时扩展主要聚焦于链深度、样本广度和学习式步骤评分器（PRMs）三个维度，而语言监督的粒度（critique granularity）作为第四个维度尚未被充分探索。

Method: VPS 是一个无需训练的框架，通过更强监督者提供的结构化自然语言批评来指导 LLM 在迭代式生成-批评-优化循环中运行，最多达 R 轮。

Result: 1) GPQA Diamond 上 GPT-5.4 组合达 94.9%（R=4），超越 94.1% 的最优基线；2) AIME 2025 上弱执行者从 11.7-26.7% 提升至 63.3-90.0%（最高+63.3点）；3) 相同算力下比 Reflexion 高 +8.5 至 +12.1 点，比 Self-Consistency@5 在 GPQA 上高 +5.0pp、LiveCodeBench 上高 +8.3pp；4) 性能与监督者-执行者能力差距强正相关（r=0.90）；5) 代码综合等误差难以语言化任务性能下降，提示需混合语言-可执行方法。

Conclusion: 批评粒度是推理时扩展的新的有效维度。VPS 无需梯度更新即可显著提升推理能力，但误差不可语言化时需结合可执行验证方法。

Abstract: Inference-time scaling for LLM reasoning has focused on three axes: chain depth, sample breadth, and learned step-scorers (PRMs). We introduce a fourth axis, granularity of external verbal supervision, via Verbal Process Supervision (VPS), a training-free framework that uses structured natural-language critique from a stronger supervisor to guide an iterative generate-critique-refine loop up to a round budget R. Across GPQA Diamond, AIME 2025, and LiveCodeBench V6 (covering both closed and open models), VPS yields three key results. First, on GPQA Diamond, GPT-5.4 (High) | GPT-5.4 (Low) reaches 94.9% at R=4, surpassing the 94.1% state of the art without gradient updates. Second, on AIME 2025, VPS enables strong weak-actor rescue, boosting scores from 11.7-26.7% to 63.3-90.0% (up to +63.3 points). Third, at matched compute, VPS outperforms Reflexion by +8.5 to +12.1 points and Self-Consistency@5 by +5.0 pp (GPQA) and +8.3 pp (LiveCodeBench), isolating critique granularity as the key driver. Performance scales with the supervisor-actor capability gap (Pearson r=0.90) and degrades when errors are not linguistically expressible (e.g., code synthesis), motivating hybrid verbal-executable methods. These results establish critique granularity as a new axis of inference-time scaling.

</details>

*Olufunke O. Sarumi,Charles Welch,Daniel Braun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Summary generation failed

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Beyond exploring disaggregated labels for modeling perspectives, annotator rationales provide fine-grained signals of individual perspectives. In this work, we propose a framework for jointly modeling annotator-specific label prediction and corresponding explanations, fine-tuned on the annotators' provided rationales. Using a dataset with disaggregated natural language inference (NLI) annotations and annotator-provided explanations, we condition predictions on both annotator identity and demographic metadata through a representation-level User Passport mechanism. We further introduce two explainer architectures: a post-hoc prompt-based explainer and a prefixed bridge explainer that transfers annotator-conditioned classifier representations directly into a generative model. This design enables explanation generation aligned with individual annotator perspectives. Our results show that incorporating explanation modeling substantially improves predictive performance over a baseline annotator-aware classifier, with the prefixed bridge approach achieving more stable label alignment and higher semantic consistency, while the post-hoc approach yields stronger lexical similarity. These findings indicate that modeling explanations as expressions of fine-grained perspective provides a richer and more faithful representation of disagreement. The proposed approaches advance perspectivist modeling by integrating annotator-specific rationales into both predictive and generative components.

</details>


### [45] [Fixation Sequences as Time Series: A Topological Approach to Dyslexia Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21698)
*Marius Huber,David R. Reich,Lena A. Jäger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 应用拓扑数据分析中的持续同调方法，通过开发新的滤流技术将眼动追踪数据（注视序列）转化为时间序列分析，构建混合模型，结合拓扑特征与传统统计特征进行阅读障碍检测，在Copenhagen语料库上验证了方法的有效性，拓扑特征能够捕获传统方法遗漏的互补信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Traditional eye-tracking analysis methods for dyslexia detection rely on limited features that may not capture the full structure of fixation sequences.

Method: Developed novel filtrations for time series using persistent homology; interpreted fixation sequences as time series; constructed hybrid models combining topological features with traditional statistical features.

Result: Hybrid models combining topological and statistical features outperform approaches using only traditional features; topological features achieve comparable performance to established baselines; proposed novel filtrations outperform existing ones.

Conclusion: Novel filtrations for persistent homology improve performance; topological features effectively capture complementary information in fixation sequences for dyslexia detection.

Abstract: Persistent homology, a method from topological data analysis, extracts robust, multi-scale features from data. It produces stable representations of time series by applying varying thresholds to their values (a process known as a \textit{filtration}). We develop novel filtrations for time series and introduce topological methods for the analysis of eye-tracking data, by interpreting fixation sequences as time series, and constructing ``hybrid models'' that combine topological features with traditional statistical features. We empirically evaluate our method by applying it to the task of dyslexia detection from eye-tracking-while-reading data using the Copenhagen Corpus, which contains scanpaths from dyslexic and non-dyslexic L1 and L2 readers. Our hybrid models outperform existing approaches that rely solely on traditional features, showing that persistent homology captures complementary information encoded in fixation sequences. The strength of these topological features is further underscored by their achieving performance comparable to established baseline methods. Importantly, our proposed filtrations outperform existing ones.

</details>


### [46] [Phonological Subspace Collapse Is Aetiology-Specific and Cross-Lingually Stable: Evidence from 3,374 Speakers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21706)
*Bernard Muller,Antonio Armando Ortiz Barrañón,LaVonne Roberts*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究在大规模跨语言、跨病因数据集上验证了基于自监督语音表征的音韵特征子空间 d-prime 可分性方法用于构音障碍严重度评估的可行性，发现该方法在组间层面具有高区分性、跨语言 profile 形状稳定性以及跨模型架构鲁棒性，但个体层面分类性能仍有待提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将先前仅在 890 名说话者、5 种语言上验证的训练无关构音障碍严重度评估方法扩展至更大规模、更多样化的数据集，以验证其在不同病因、语言和模型架构下的泛化能力。

Method: 在 25 个数据集、12 种语言、3374 名说话人中展开研究，覆盖 5 种病因（帕金森病、脑瘫、肌萎缩侧索硬化症、唐氏综合征、脑卒中）及健康对照组，采用 6 种 SSL backbone，通过 d-prime 可分性、余弦相似度、效应量（epsilon-squared、Cohen's d）等指标进行分析，并进行固定 token 数的验证实验。

Result: （1）组间层面：13 个音韵特征中 10 个达到大效应量（epsilon-squared>0.14，Holm校正 p<0.001），帕金森病与运动执行障碍组可区分（Cohen's d=0.83），但个体层面分类有限（macro F1仅 22.6%）；（2）跨语言：5 维辅音 d-prime profile 余弦相似度>0.95，profile 形状具跨语言稳定性；（3）跨 backbone：6 种模型均产生单调严重度梯度，模型间一致性 rho>0.77；固定 token d-prime 估计仍保持 rho=-0.733 的严重度相关性，排除 token 计数伪影。

Conclusion: phonological subspace analysis可以作为一种稳健的、无需训练的框架，用于病因感知的构音障碍表征，且在跨语言 profile 形状稳定性和跨 backbone 鲁棒性方面均表现出良好效果。该方法支持语言无关的退化模式表型分析，但绝对严重度解读仍需语料库内校准。

Abstract: We previously introduced a training-free method for dysarthria severity assessment based on d-prime separability of phonological feature subspaces in frozen self-supervised speech representations, validated on 890 speakers across 5 languages with HuBERT-base. Here, we scale the analysis to 3,374 speakers from 25 datasets spanning 12 languages and 5 aetiologies (Parkinson's disease, cerebral palsy, ALS, Down syndrome, and stroke), plus healthy controls, using 6 SSL backbones. We report three findings. First, aetiology-specific degradation profiles are distinguishable at the group level: 10 of 13 features yield large effect sizes (epsilon-squared > 0.14, Holm-corrected p < 0.001), with Parkinson's disease separable from the articulatory execution group at Cohen's d = 0.83; individual-level classification remains limited (22.6% macro F1). Second, profiles show cross-lingual profile-shape stability: cosine similarity of 5-dimensional consonant d-prime profiles exceeds 0.95 across the languages available for each aetiology. Absolute d-prime magnitudes are not cross-lingually calibrated, so the method supports language-independent phenotyping of degradation patterns but requires within-corpus calibration for absolute severity interpretation. Third, the method is architecture-independent: all 6 backbones produce monotonic severity gradients with inter-model agreement exceeding rho = 0.77. Fixed-token d-prime estimation preserves the severity correlation (rho = -0.733 at 200 tokens per class), confirming that the signal is not a token-count artefact. These results support phonological subspace analysis as a robust, training-free framework for aetiology-aware dysarthria characterisation, with evidence of cross-lingual profile-shape stability and cross-backbone robustness in the represented sample.

</details>


### [47] [Beyond N-gram: Data-Aware X-GRAM Extraction for Efficient Embedding Parameter Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21724)
*Yilong Chen,Yanxi Xie,Zitian Gao,He Xin,Yihao Xiao,Renbiao Liu,Haoming Luo,Yifan Luo,Zhengmao Ye,Tingwen Liu,Xin Zhao,Ran Tao,Bryan Dai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: X-GRAM is a frequency-aware dynamic token-injection framework that addresses parameter inefficiency and memory growth in memory-augmented models through hybrid hashing, alias mixing, and depth-aware gating, achieving significant accuracy improvements with smaller memory footprint.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Large token-indexed lookup tables provide compute-decoupled scaling but suffer from poor parameter efficiency and rapid memory growth due to three root causes: Zipfian under-training of the long tail, heterogeneous demand across layers, and "slot collapse" that produces redundant embeddings.

Method: X-GRAM employs hybrid hashing and alias mixing to compress the tail while preserving head capacity, refines retrieved vectors via normalized SwiGLU ShortConv to extract diverse local n-gram features, and integrates signals into attention value streams and inter-layer residuals using depth-aware gating, introducing a memory-centric scaling axis that decouples model capacity from FLOPs.

Result: Extensive evaluations at 0.73B and 1.15B scales show X-GRAM improves average accuracy by up to 4.4 points over the vanilla backbone and 3.2 points over strong retrieval baselines, while using substantially smaller tables in the 50% configuration.

Conclusion: X-GRAM offers a scalable and practical paradigm for future memory-augmented architectures by decoupling capacity from compute through efficient memory management. Code is available at https://github.com/Longyichen/X-gram.

Abstract: Large token-indexed lookup tables provide a compute-decoupled scaling path, but their practical gains are often limited by poor parameter efficiency and rapid memory growth. We attribute these limitations to Zipfian under-training of the long tail, heterogeneous demand across layers, and "slot collapse" that produces redundant embeddings. To address this, we propose X-GRAM, a frequency-aware dynamic token-injection framework. X-GRAM employs hybrid hashing and alias mixing to compress the tail while preserving head capacity, and refines retrieved vectors via normalized SwiGLU ShortConv to extract diverse local n-gram features. These signals are integrated into attention value streams and inter-layer residuals using depth-aware gating, effectively aligning static memory with dynamic context. This design introduces a memory-centric scaling axis that decouples model capacity from FLOPs. Extensive evaluations at the 0.73B and 1.15B scales show that X-GRAM improves average accuracy by as much as 4.4 points over the vanilla backbone and 3.2 points over strong retrieval baselines, while using substantially smaller tables in the 50% configuration. Overall, by decoupling capacity from compute through efficient memory management, X-GRAM offers a scalable and practical paradigm for future memory-augmented architectures. Code aviliable in https://github.com/Longyichen/X-gram.

</details>


### [48] [AEL: Agent Evolving Learning for Open-Ended Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21725)
*Wujiang Xu,Jiaojiao Han,Minghao Guo,Kai Mei,Xi Zhu,Han Zhang,Dimitris N. Metaxas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Agent Evolving Learning (AEL)框架，通过双时间尺度机制解决LLM智能体在开放环境中无法有效利用历史经验的问题。快时间尺度用Thompson Sampling bandits选择记忆检索策略，慢时间尺度用LLM驱动的反思诊断失败模式。在208个episode的金融组合基准测试中，Sharpe比率达2.13±0.47，优于5个已发表的自改进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在开放环境中执行数百个连续任务时仍基本无状态，无法将过去经验转化为未来行为的改进。核心障碍不是"记住什么"，而是"如何使用"所记住的内容，包括：选择何种检索策略、如何解释先验结果、以及当前策略本身何时需要改变。

Method: 提出双时间尺度框架AEL：1) 快时间尺度：Thompson Sampling bandits学习每个episode应使用何种记忆检索策略；2) 慢时间尺度：LLM驱动的反思诊断失败模式并将因果洞察注入决策prompt，提供解读检索证据的框架。消融实验包含9个变体，测试了规划器演化、工具级选择、冷启动初始化、技能提取和三种信用分配方法。

Result: 在10个行业多样化股票、208 episodes、5个随机种子的序列组合基准上，AEL达到Sharpe比率2.13±0.47，优于5个已发表的自改进方法和所有非LLM基线，且在所有LLM方法中保持最低方差。消融实验揭示"少即是多"模式：记忆+反思组合相比无状态基线产生58%的累积改进，但所有额外机制（规划器演化、工具级选择、冷启动初始化、技能提取、三种信用分配方法）均导致性能下降。

Conclusion: 智能体自我改进的瓶颈在于"自我诊断如何使用经验"而非增加架构复杂性。记忆与反思的协同是必要且充分的，额外机制反而可能引入噪声或过度复杂化。这一发现对设计实用LLM智能体系统具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly operate in open-ended environments spanning hundreds of sequential episodes, yet they remain largely stateless: each task is solved from scratch without converting past experience into better future behavior. The central obstacle is not \emph{what} to remember but \emph{how to use} what has been remembered, including which retrieval policy to apply, how to interpret prior outcomes, and when the current strategy itself must change. We introduce \emph{Agent Evolving Learning} (\ael{}), a two-timescale framework that addresses this obstacle. At the fast timescale, a Thompson Sampling bandit learns which memory retrieval policy to apply at each episode; at the slow timescale, LLM-driven reflection diagnoses failure patterns and injects causal insights into the agent's decision prompt, giving it an interpretive frame for the evidence it retrieves. On a sequential portfolio benchmark (10 sector-diverse tickers, 208 episodes, 5 random seeds), \ael{} achieves a Sharpe ratio of 2.13$\pm$0.47, outperforming five published self-improving methods and all non-LLM baselines while maintaining the lowest variance among all LLM-based approaches. A nine-variant ablation reveals a ``less is more'' pattern: memory and reflection together produce a 58\% cumulative improvement over the stateless baseline, yet every additional mechanism we test (planner evolution, per-tool selection, cold-start initialization, skill extraction, and three credit assignment methods) \emph{degrades} performance. This demonstrates that the bottleneck in agent self-improvement is \emph{self-diagnosing how to use} experience rather than adding architectural complexity. Code and data: https://github.com/WujiangXu/AEL.

</details>


### [49] [AUDITA: A New Dataset to Audit Humans vs. AI Skill at Audio QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21766)
*Tasnim Kabir,Dmytro Kurdydyk,Aadi Palnitkar,Liam Dorn,Ahmed Haj Ahmed,Jordan Lee Boyd-Graber*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AUDITA是一个用于评估音频推理能力的大规模真实世界基准测试，旨在超越表面声学识别，要求模型进行真正的音频推理。当前最优模型的准确率低于8.86%，而人类平均准确率为32.13%，表明现有模型存在系统性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频问答基准测试主要关注声音事件分类或基于标题的查询，使模型能够通过捷径策略（如短时提示、词汇先验、数据集偏差）或绕过音频直接使用元数据和标题来成功回答，而非进行真正的推理。

Method: 构建AUDITA基准测试，包含精心策划的人类撰写的 trivia 问题，基于真实世界音频设计，通过具有挑战性的干扰项、长程时间依赖性和无法从孤立文本或声音线索单独回答的探测查询来测试鲁棒的听觉推理能力。此外，应用项目反应理论(IRT)评估潜在能力、问题难度并揭示模型和数据的系统性缺陷。

Result: 人类平均准确率为32.13%，表明任务具有挑战性但人类能够进行有意义的理解。最先进的音频问答模型表现极差，平均准确率低于8.86%。

Conclusion: AUDITA有效揭示了现有音频问答模型缺乏真正的音频推理能力，该基准测试可作为评估模型音频理解能力的重要工具，IRR分析进一步暴露了模型和数据层面的系统性缺陷。

Abstract: Existing audio question answering benchmarks largely emphasize sound event classification or caption-grounded queries, often enabling models to succeed through shortcut strategies, short-duration cues, lexical priors, dataset-specific biases, or even bypassing audio via metadata and captions rather than genuine reasoning Thus, we present AUDITA (Audio Understanding from Diverse Internet Trivia Authors), a large-scale, real-world benchmark to rigorously evaluate audio reasoning beyond surface-level acoustic recognition. AUDITA comprises carefully curated, human-authored trivia questions grounded in real-world audio, designed to stress robust auditory reasoning through challenging distractors and long-range temporal dependencies, using probing queries that cannot be answered from isolated text or sound cues alone. Human average accuracy of 32.13% shows both the challenge of the task while demonstrating meaningful comprehension of the audio. In stark contrast, state of-the-art audio question answering models perform poorly, with average accuracy below 8.86%. Beyond raw accuracy, we apply Item Response Theory (IRT) to estimate latent proficiency, question difficulty, and expose systematic deficiencies of the models and data.

</details>


### [50] [SemEval-2026 Task 4: Narrative Story Similarity and Narrative Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21782)
*Hans Ole Hatzel,Ekaterina Artemova,Haimo Paul Stiemer,Evelyn Gius,Chris Biemann*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: NSNRL共享任务将叙事相似性定义为二分类问题（判断两故事中哪个与锚点故事更相似），收集了1000+故事三元组的标注数据，46个团队提交71份系统。LLM集成在分类任务中表现最佳，而预训练嵌入模型的预处理/后处理方案与微调方案性能相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化叙事相似性评估缺乏统一的理论基础和数据标注标准，现有方法难以捕捉叙事的深层结构特征。需要一个兼容叙事理论与人类直觉的叙事相似性定义，并建立标准化的评估基准。

Method: 提出兼容叙事理论与直觉判断的新型叙事相似性定义；采用三元组分类范式，将相似性比较转化为二元选择问题；构建包含1000+故事三元组的数据集，每条数据至少2个标注且标注者间达成一致。任务分为三元组分类和嵌入表示两个赛道。

Result: 收到46个团队的71份最终提交。三元组分类赛道中，LLM集成方案占据多数高分位置；嵌入表示赛道中，预训练模型的预处理/后处理方案与定制微调方案性能相当。分析显示两个赛道均存在改进空间。

Conclusion: 该共享任务为叙事相似性研究提供了标准化评估框架和数据资源，验证了当前方法在叙事理解任务上的局限性，为未来改进指明了方向。

Abstract: We present the shared task on narrative similarity and narrative representation learning - NSNRL (pronounced "nass-na-rel"). The task operationalizes narrative similarity as a binary classification problem: determining which of two stories is more similar to an anchor story. We introduce a novel definition of narrative similarity, compatible with both narrative theory and intuitive judgment. Based on the similarity judgments collected under this concept, we also evaluate narrative embedding representations. We collected at least two annotations each for more than 1,000 story summary triples, with each annotation being backed by at least two annotators in agreement. This paper describes the sampling and annotation process for the dataset; further, we give an overview of the submitted systems and the techniques they employ. We received a total of 71 final submissions from 46 teams across our two tracks. In our triple-based classification setup, LLM ensembles make up many of the top-scoring systems, while in the embedding setup, systems with pre- and post-processing on pretrained embedding models perform about on par with custom fine-tuned solutions. Our analysis identifies potential headroom for improvement of automated systems in both tracks. The task website includes visualizations of embeddings alongside instance-level classification results for all teams.

</details>


### [51] [Machine Behavior in Relational Moral Dilemmas: Moral Rightness, Predicted Human Behavior, and Model Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21871)
*Jiseon Kim,Jea Kwon,Luiz Felipe Vecchietti,Wenchao Dong,Jaehong Kim,Meeyoung Cha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过"告密者困境"实验，探讨大语言模型(LLM)是否具备人类社会情境中的道德判断能力，发现模型在自主决策时倾向于遵循刚性规范而非考虑人际关系的复杂性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM日益成为决策支持系统，理解其是否编码人类道德判断的社会性维度变得至关重要。人类道德判断具有情境依赖性且受人际关系影响，但这种社会敏感性是否存在于LLM中尚不明确。

Method: 采用"告密者困境"(Whistleblower's Dilemma)实验范式，操纵两个实验维度：犯罪严重程度和关系亲密度。研究从三个视角评估模型：(1)道德正当性(规定性规范)；(2)预测人类行为(描述性社会期望)；(3)自主模型决策。通过分析推理过程进行跨视角比较。

Result: 发现明显的跨视角分歧：道德正当性判断始终以公平为导向，而预测的人类行为随关系亲密度增加显著转向忠诚导向。关键发现：模型自主决策与道德正当性判断一致，而非与其自身的行为预测一致。

Conclusion: LLM决策优先考虑刚性规定性规则，而非其内部世界模型中存在的社会敏感性。这种不一致性可能导致现实部署中的重大对齐失误。

Abstract: Human moral judgment is context-dependent and modulated by interpersonal relationships. As large language models (LLMs) increasingly function as decision-support systems, determining whether they encode these social nuances is critical. We characterize machine behavior using the Whistleblower's Dilemma by varying two experimental dimensions: crime severity and relational closeness. Our study evaluates three distinct perspectives: (1) moral rightness (prescriptive norms), (2) predicted human behavior (descriptive social expectations), and (3) autonomous model decision-making. By analyzing the reasoning processes, we identify a clear cross-perspective divergence: while moral rightness remains consistently fairness-oriented, predicted human behavior shifts significantly toward loyalty as relational closeness increases. Crucially, model decisions align with moral rightness judgments rather than their own behavioral predictions. This inconsistency suggests that LLM decision-making prioritizes rigid, prescriptive rules over the social sensitivity present in their internal world-modeling, which poses a gap that may lead to significant misalignments in real-world deployments.

</details>


### [52] [Revisiting Non-Verbatim Memorization in Large Language Models: The Role of Entity Surface Forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21882)
*Yuto Nishida,Naoki Shikoda,Yosuke Kishinami,Ryo Fujii,Makoto Morishita,Hidetaka Kamigaito,Taro Watanabe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出RedirectQA数据集研究LLM的非逐字记忆，发现模型预测一致性随表面形式类别变化，强调评估中表面形式多样性的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的实体QA评估使用单一规范表面形式查询，难以将事实记忆与特定名称的访问区分开来。

Method: 提出RedirectQA数据集，利用Wikipedia重定向信息将Wikidata事实三元组与各类实体表面形式（包括别名、缩写、拼写变体和常见错误形式）关联；在13个LLM上测试表面形式的条件化事实记忆。

Result: 预测结果常随表面形式变化而改变；模型对轻微正字法变体更鲁棒，对别名和缩写等较大词汇变体较脆弱；实体频率和表面频率均与准确性相关，且实体频率的贡献常超出表面频率。

Conclusion: 事实记忆既非纯粹的形式特定，也非完全的形式不变；表面形式多样性在评估非逐字记忆方面具有重要意义。

Abstract: Understanding what kinds of factual knowledge large language models (LLMs) memorize is essential for evaluating their reliability and limitations. Entity-based QA is a common framework for analyzing non-verbatim memorization, but typical evaluations query each entity using a single canonical surface form, making it difficult to disentangle fact memorization from access through a particular name. We introduce RedirectQA, an entity-based QA dataset that uses Wikipedia redirect information to associate Wikidata factual triples with categorized surface forms for each entity, including alternative names, abbreviations, spelling variants, and common erroneous forms. Across 13 LLMs, we examine surface-conditioned factual memorization and find that prediction outcomes often change when only the entity surface form changes. This inconsistency is category-dependent: models are more robust to minor orthographic variations than to larger lexical variations such as aliases and abbreviations. Frequency analyses further suggest that both entity- and surface-level frequencies are associated with accuracy, and that entity frequency often contributes beyond surface frequency. Overall, factual memorization appears neither purely surface-specific nor fully surface-invariant, highlighting the importance of surface-form diversity in evaluating non-verbatim memorization.

</details>


### [53] [A Multimodal Text- and Graph-Based Approach for Open-Domain Event Extraction from Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21885)
*Praval Sharma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出MODEE方法，将图学习与LLM文本表示结合，解决开放域事件抽取中文档级上下文推理难题，在开放域和封闭域场景均超越现有最佳方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件抽取方法存在局限：(1)封闭域方法受限于预定义事件类型，难以泛化到未见类型；(2)开放域方法虽能处理无约束事件类型，但未充分利用大语言模型能力；(3)文档级上下文、结构化和语义推理对有效事件抽取至关重要，但LLM因"lost-in-the-middle"和注意力稀释现象难以胜任。

Method: 提出MODEE（多模态开放域事件抽取）方法，将基于图的学习与LLM文本表示相结合来建模文档级推理能力。

Result: 在大规模数据集上的实证评估表明，MODEE优于最先进的开放域事件抽取方法，并能泛化到封闭域事件抽取场景，性能超越现有算法。

Conclusion: MODEE通过融合图学习与LLM表示，有效解决了开放域事件抽取的文档级推理问题，具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Event extraction is essential for event understanding and analysis. It supports tasks such as document summarization and decision-making in emergency scenarios. However, existing event extraction approaches have limitations: (1) closed-domain algorithms are restricted to predefined event types and thus rarely generalize to unseen types and (2) open-domain event extraction algorithms, capable of handling unconstrained event types, have largely overlooked the potential of large language models (LLMs) despite their advanced abilities. Additionally, they do not explicitly model document-level contextual, structural, and semantic reasoning, which are crucial for effective event extraction but remain challenging for LLMs due to lost-in-the-middle phenomenon and attention dilution. To address these limitations, we propose multimodal open-domain event extraction, MODEE , a novel approach for open-domain event extraction that combines graph-based learning with text-based representation from LLMs to model document-level reasoning. Empirical evaluations on large datasets demonstrate that MODEE outperforms state-of-the-art open-domain event extraction approaches and can be generalized to closed-domain event extraction, where it outperforms existing algorithms.

</details>


### [54] [TingIS: Real-time Risk Event Discovery from Noisy Customer Incidents at Enterprise Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21889)
*Jun Wang,Ziyin Zhang,Rui Wang,Hang Yu,Peng Di,Rui Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Real-time detection and mitigation of technical anomalies are critical for large-scale cloud-native services, where even minutes of downtime can result in massive financial losses and diminished user trust. While customer incidents serve as a vital signal for discovering risks missed by monitoring, extracting actionable intelligence from this data remains challenging due to extreme noise, high throughput, and semantic complexity of diverse business lines. In this paper, we present TingIS, an end-to-end system designed for enterprise-grade incident discovery. At the core of TingIS is a multi-stage event linking engine that synergizes efficient indexing techniques with Large Language Models (LLMs) to make informed decisions on event merging, enabling the stable extraction of actionable incidents from just a handful of diverse user descriptions. This engine is complemented by a cascaded routing mechanism for precise business attribution and a multi-dimensional noise reduction pipeline that integrates domain knowledge, statistical patterns, and behavioral filtering. Deployed in a production environment handling a peak throughput of over 2,000 messages per minute and 300,000 messages per day, TingIS achieves a P90 alert latency of 3.5 minutes and a 95\% discovery rate for high-priority incidents. Benchmarks constructed from real-world data demonstrate that TingIS significantly outperforms baseline methods in routing accuracy, clustering quality, and Signal-to-Noise Ratio.

</details>


### [55] [Mango: Multi-Agent Web Navigation via Global-View Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18779)
*Weixi Tong,Yifeng Di,Tianyi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Mango是一个多智能体网页导航方法，通过多臂老虎机问题和Thompson Sampling自适应分配导航预算，结合情景记忆组件存储导航历史，解决现有智能体从根URL探索导致效率低下的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网页智能体通常从根URL开始探索，对于深层层级结构的复杂网站效率低下，容易陷入导航陷阱或无法在有限预算内到达目标信息。

Method: 1) 将URL选择建模为多臂老虎机问题；2) 使用Thompson Sampling自适应分配候选URL间的导航预算；3) 引入情景记忆组件存储导航历史，实现从先前尝试中学习。

Result: WebVoyager上使用GPT-5-mini达到63.6%成功率，超越最佳基线7.3%；WebWalkerQA上达到52.5%成功率，超越最佳基线26.8%；在开源和闭源模型上均展现通用性。

Conclusion: 通过结构感知和自适应预算分配机制，Mango能有效提升复杂网站上的导航效率，且具有良好的模型通用性。

Abstract: Existing web agents typically initiate exploration from the root URL, which is inefficient for complex websites with deep hierarchical structures. Without a global view of the website's structure, agents frequently fall into navigation traps, explore irrelevant branches, or fail to reach target information within a limited budget. We propose Mango, a multi-agent web navigation method that leverages the website structure to dynamically determine optimal starting points. We formulate URL selection as a multi-armed bandit problem and employ Thompson Sampling to adaptively allocate the navigation budget across candidate URLs. Furthermore, we introduce an episodic memory component to store navigation history, enabling the agent to learn from previous attempts. Experiments on WebVoyager demonstrate that Mango achieves a success rate of 63.6% when using GPT-5-mini, outperforming the best baseline by 7.3%. Furthermore, on WebWalkerQA, Mango attains a 52.5% success rate, surpassing the best baseline by 26.8%. We also demonstrate the generalizability of Mango using both open-source and closed-source models as backbones. Our data and code are open-source and available at https://github.com/VichyTong/Mango.

</details>


### [56] [EVENT5Ws: A Large Dataset for Open-Domain Event Extraction from Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21890)
*Praval Sharma,Ashok Samal,Leen-Kiat Soh,Deepti Joshi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文创建了EVENT5Ws，一个大规模的开放域事件抽取数据集，用于解决现有数据集在事件类型覆盖和人工标注质量方面的局限性，并评估了最新预训练语言模型在该任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件抽取数据集存在两个主要问题：(1) 封闭域设置下事件类型覆盖有限；(2) 开放域设置下缺乏大规模、人工验证的数据集。这些限制阻碍了自动化事件抽取方法的进一步发展。

Method: 设计了系统化的标注流程来构建数据集，对标注复杂性进行了实证分析，并使用EVENT5Ws评估了最先进的预训练大语言模型。

Result: EVENT5Ws是一个大规模、手动标注且经过统计验证的开放域事件抽取数据集；在该数据集上建立了基准测试；基于该数据集训练的模型能够有效泛化到不同地理背景的数据集。

Conclusion: EVENT5Ws数据集能够支持可泛化算法的开发；研究总结了数据集开发过程中的经验教训，为未来大规模数据集开发提供了建议。

Abstract: Event extraction identifies the central aspects of events from text. It supports event understanding and analysis, which is crucial for tasks such as informed decision-making in emergencies. Therefore, it is necessary to develop automated event extraction approaches. However, existing datasets for algorithm development have limitations, including limited coverage of event types in closed-domain settings and a lack of large, manually verified dataset in open-domain settings. To address these limitations, we create EVENT5Ws , a large, manually annotated, and statistically verified open-domain event extraction dataset. We design a systematic annotation pipeline to create the dataset and provide empirical insights into annotation complexity. Using EVENT5Ws, we evaluate state-of-the-art pre-trained large language models and establish a benchmark for future research. We further show that models trained on EVENT5Ws generalize effectively to datasets from different geographical contexts, which demonstrates its potential for developing generalizable algorithms. Finally, we summarize the lessons learned during the dataset development and provide recommendations to support future large-scale dataset development.

</details>


### [57] [GiVA: Gradient-Informed Bases for Vector-Based Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21901)
*Neeraj Gangwar,Rishabh Deshmukh,Michael Shavlovsky,Hancao Li,Vivek Mittal,Lexing Ying,Nickvash Kani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GiVA是一种基于梯度初始化的向量适配方法，在保持向量适配极端参数效率的同时，实现了与LoRA相当的训练时间，并将秩需求降低8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着模型规模不断增大，参数高效微调成为重要研究方向。LoRA虽被广泛采用，但基于向量的适配方法虽然参数效率更高，却需要更高的秩来匹配LoRA性能，导致训练成本增加。

Method: 提出GiVA，一种针对向量适配的梯度初始化策略。该方法通过改进初始化方式，而非改变模型结构或优化目标，来提升向量适配方法的性能。

Result: GiVA在自然语言理解、自然语言生成和图像分类等多任务上均取得优异表现。与现有向量适配方法和LoRA相比，性能相当或更优，同时将秩需求降低8倍。

Conclusion: GiVA成功融合了向量适配的参数效率和LoRA的训练效率，为大模型参数高效微调提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: As model sizes continue to grow, parameter-efficient fine-tuning has emerged as a powerful alternative to full fine-tuning. While LoRA is widely adopted among these methods, recent research has explored vector-based adaptation methods due to their extreme parameter efficiency. However, these methods typically require substantially higher ranks than LoRA to match its performance, leading to increased training costs. This work introduces GiVA, a gradient-based initialization strategy for vector-based adaptation. It achieves training times comparable to LoRA and maintains the extreme parameter efficiency of vector-based adaptation. We evaluate GiVA across diverse benchmarks, including natural language understanding, natural language generation, and image classification. Experiments show that our approach consistently outperforms or achieves performance competitive with existing vector-based adaptation methods and LoRA while reducing rank requirements by a factor of eight ($8\times$).

</details>


### [58] [Evaluation of Automatic Speech Recognition Using Generative Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21928)
*Thibault Bañeras-Roux,Shashi Kumar,Driss Khalil,Sergio Burdisso,Petr Motlicek,Shiran Liu,Mickael Rouvier,Jane Wottawa,Richard Dufour*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探索了基于解码器的大语言模型（LLM）在自动语音识别（ASR）评估中的潜力，提出三种评估方法，并在HATS数据集上验证了LLM可实现92-94%的人类标注一致性，显著优于传统WER指标（63%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ASR评估指标WER仅关注词汇表面错误，忽略语义层面的准确性，无法有效反映人类对转录质量的感知。现有基于编码器的语义嵌入指标表现优于WER，但解码器LLM在这一任务上的潜力尚未被充分探索。

Method: 研究采用三种方法评估LLM在ASR评估中的适用性：（1）假设选择任务：在两个候选转录中挑选更优者；（2）语义距离计算：利用LLM生成式嵌入计算语义差异；（3）定性错误分类：通过LLM对错误类型进行可解释性分类。实验基于HATS数据集进行评估。

Result: 最佳LLM在假设选择任务上达到92-94%的人类标注一致性，远超WER的63%，同时优于现有语义度量指标。解码器LLM生成的嵌入性能与编码器模型相当，证明了其在语义评估方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 解码器LLM为ASR评估提供了一条有前景的方向，能够实现更高的语义相关性评估，同时具备可解释性和语义理解能力，为下一代ASR评估框架奠定了基础。

Abstract: Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) is traditionally evaluated using Word Error Rate (WER), a metric that is insensitive to meaning. Embedding-based semantic metrics are better correlated with human perception, but decoder-based Large Language Models (LLMs) remain underexplored for this task. This paper evaluates their relevance through three approaches: (1) selecting the best hypothesis between two candidates, (2) computing semantic distance using generative embeddings, and (3) qualitative classification of errors. On the HATS dataset, the best LLMs achieve 92--94\% agreement with human annotators for hypothesis selection, compared to 63\% for WER, also outperforming semantic metrics. Embeddings from decoder-based LLMs show performance comparable to encoder models. Finally, LLMs offer a promising direction for interpretable and semantic ASR evaluation.

</details>

### [59] [Sibling Rivalry in the Ivory Tower: Mass Science, Expanding Scholarly Families, and the Reshaping of Academic Stratification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20864)
*Likun Cao,Jie Hua,James Evans*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文将人口学中的"出生顺序"概念引入科学社会学研究，发现同一导师的后续博士生在各成就维度上系统性落后于早期学生，揭示了科学分层的新机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要依赖马太效应框架解释科学分层，但该框架在大众科学和团队研究主导时代解释力有限。尽管科学家共享机构和学术谱系，职业成就差异仍难以解释，需要新的理论视角。

Method: ①借鉴人口学中家庭作为基本繁殖单位的概念，将学术谱系类比为生物家庭；②将"出生顺序"概念适配到博士生群体，分析其在谱系中的序列位置如何影响职业轨迹；③使用超过100万名美国博士毕业生的纵向数据进行实证分析。

Result: ①同一导师的后续博士生在多个成就维度上系统性地落后于早期学生；②导师对所有学生投入资源相当；③后续学生从成熟学者获得的认知刺激较少；④后续学生被迫在更狭窄的细分领域发展。

Conclusion: 本文提出的人口学框架为理解科学分层提供了新视角，证明了人口学概念可有效分析更广泛的社会和认识系统，后续学生的认知刺激减少和领域窄化共同制约其智力发展与职业成功。

Abstract: This paper investigates mechanisms underlying scientific stratification in the transition from elite to mass science. Existing scholarship has examined stratification through the Matthew effect framework, but this approach is increasingly limited as mass, team-based research becomes dominant. While scientists now share institutions and lineages, substantial career outcome differences remain unexplained. We propose integrating demographic concepts into science studies. Drawing parallels between biological families and scholarly lineages as fundamental reproductive units, we adapt the birth order concept to examine how doctoral student sequence within a lineage shapes career trajectories. Using data on over one million U.S. doctoral graduates, we find that later students of the same advisor systematically underperform earlier ones across multiple achievement dimensions, both short and long term. Examining underlying mechanisms reveals that although advisors invest comparable resources in all students, later students receive less cognitive stimulation from mature scholars than peers and specialize in narrower niches under peer differentiation pressure. Both of these factors constrain intellectual development and subsequent success. By introducing a demographic framework, this paper offers new perspectives on scientific stratification and demonstrates how demographic concepts can fruitfully analyze broader social and epistemic systems.

</details>


### [60] [Advances in Art: Orthogonal Disruption and the Beauty in Schematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20865)
*Sergio Alvarez-Telena,Marta Diez-Fernandez*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该文提出"正交艺术"（Orthogonal Art）概念，将其定位为对生成式AI的辩证性回应而非工具化服务，并以技术图纸为媒介的具体实践作为框架的首个实例化方案，同时论证了其在促进人文与工程/哲学跨学科素养方面的教学价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI辅助创意实践日益盛行的背景下，艺术面临被AI工具化的风险。研究者认为有必要探索一种与AI形成"正交"（非平行、非从属）关系的艺术实践——既非服务于AI，亦非被动接受AI辅助，而是在AI能力边界之外开辟独立的生成与概念空间。

Method: 采用概念建构与实例化相结合的研究路径：以"正交性"（orthogonality）为原则界定AI无法触及的生成与概念空间，并以此作为新艺术学科的结构性定义基础；通过技术图纸作为主要媒介的具体实践，实现该框架的落地验证；同时在教学层面探索人文与工程/哲学的跨学科连接。

Result: 提出了"正交艺术"作为对AI具有结构性抵牾特征的创新学科框架，并通过以技术图纸为媒介的具体艺术实践初步验证了该框架的可行性，同时展示了其在跨学科教育（艺术、工程、哲学）中的桥梁价值。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了"正交艺术"(Orthogonal Art)作为人工智能时代艺术实践的新范式，其核心贡献在于：第一，构建了一种与AI形成结构性互补而非协作关系的艺术学科框架；第二，以技术图纸作为主要媒介实现了该框架的具体化；第三，通过将艺术实践锚定于图式逻辑与算法结构，为人文社科领域提供了进入"增强机器"(Augmented Machines)这一前沿领域的可及性路径。Orthogonal Art的提出标志着艺术领域对生成式AI的主动式抵抗与超越尝试。

Abstract: This paper introduces Orthogonal Art, a proposed artistic discipline that emerges in dialectical response to artificial intelligence rather than in service of it. Unlike AI-augmented creative practices, Orthogonal Art is structurally defined by occupying the generative and conceptual spaces that current AI systems cannot access. As a founding instantiation of this framework, the paper presents a novel artistic practice in which technical schematics serve as the primary medium. A significant secondary contribution is the pedagogical dimension of the work: by grounding artistic practice in schematic logic and algorithmic structure, the framework provides an accessible entry point into the advanced field of Augmented Machines systems, enabling cross-disciplinary literacy within Humanities at the intersection of art, engineering, and philosophy.

</details>


### [61] [Beyond the Binary: Motivations, Challenges, and Strategies of Transgender and Non-binary Software Engineering Students](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20866)
*Isabella Graßl*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: This study investigates transgender and non-binary students' experiences in software engineering education through 13 global interviews. Findings reveal that gender identity indirectly influences their career motivation (remote work appeal, desire for inclusive technology), but despite experiencing verbal hostility and discrimination from social environments—not universities—these students remain committed to their field. The paper calls for institutional support, structural changes, and increased representation.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: While software benefits from diverse design perspectives, transgender and non-binary students face unique challenges in their educational journey toward becoming software engineers. Existing research focuses on professional settings, leaving a gap in understanding the academic experiences critical for ensuring equal opportunities and preventing exclusion in the tech workforce.

Method: 13 semi-structured interviews with transgender and non-binary students in software engineering programs across the globe.

Result: Gender identity indirectly influences students' motivation to pursue software engineering through appeal of remote work and desire to create inclusive technologies. Participants experienced verbal insults, judgment, intolerance, and hostility negatively impacting mental health, despite no direct university discrimination. Challenges stem from socio-cultural norms and lack of representation. Despite obstacles, students remain committed but call for greater institutional support, structural changes, and increased representation.

Conclusion: The paper concludes with actionable recommendations for concrete institutional steps to support students, including structural changes, increased representation, and institutional support mechanisms.

Abstract: When software is designed by people from diverse identities and experiences, it is more likely to be inclusive and address a broader range of user needs. However, for transgender and non-binary students in software engineering, the path to becoming such creators may be marked by unique challenges. While existing research explores gender minorities in professional software engineering, limited attention has been given to their educational journey, a key phase for ensuring equal opportunities and preventing exclusion in the tech workforce. This study aims to address this gap by investigating the experiences of transgender and non-binary students in software engineering, with a particular focus on their motivations for entering the field, the obstacles they encounter, and potential strategies for fostering greater inclusivity within their academic environments. Based on 13 semi-structured interviews with transgender and non-binary students across the globe, we found that gender identity plays an indirect role in their decision to pursue software engineering. Key factors include the appeal of remote work and a personal desire to create more inclusive technologies. Although the participants did not report direct discrimination within their universities, many described experiencing verbal insults, judgment, intolerance, and hostility, all of which negatively impacted their mental health. These challenges often stem from socio-cultural norms and a lack of representation. Despite these obstacles, the students remain committed to their choice of study but call for greater institutional support, structural changes, and increased representation. From these findings, we suggest concrete steps to support students, regardless of gender identity.

</details>


### [62] [Preserving Decision Sovereignty in Military AI: A Trade-Secret-Safe Architectural Framework for Model Replaceability, Human Authority, and State Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20867)
*Peng Wei,Wesley Shu*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文探讨了前沿AI供应商与国家安全客户之间的结构性权力问题，核心论点认为军事AI采购的关键不仅是获得高性能模型，更是维护"决策主权"——即国家在将分析模块外包给商业供应商后，仍能保留对决策政策、版本控制、备选行为、审计追溯和最终行动授权的掌控能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着私营AI模型深度嵌入军事工作流程，供应商不仅影响技术性能，还可能影响系统的使用边界条件。Anthropic-五角大楼2026年争端、Project Maven项目及美英北约等国的政策动向表明，这一权力失衡问题已从幕后走向台前，亟需系统性的应对框架。

Method: 本文采用概念分析方法，以"能效范式"(Energetic Paradigm)为基础，构建了一种分层、模型无关的命令支持架构。该架构在避免泄露商业机密的前提下，明确区分：供应商模型作为可替代的分析组件（保持可替换性），而路由、约束、日志记录、升级机制和行动授权等关键功能始终由国家掌控。

Result: 本文提出三项核心贡献：①军事AI语境下"决策主权"的正式定义；②供应商诱发边界控制的威胁模型；③实现模型可替换性、人类权威与主权编排的公开架构规范。该架构无需披露专有实现细节，即可降低战略依赖风险。

Conclusion: 本研究为军事AI采购治理提供了理论基础，启示政策制定者：在追求AI能力的同时，必须通过架构设计确保国家保持对关键决策环节的最终控制权，这对未来联盟互操作性及跨国军事AI合作具有重要参考价值。

Abstract: Recent events surrounding the relationship between frontier AI suppliers and national-security customers have made a structural problem newly visible: once a privately governed model becomes embedded in military workflows, the supplier can influence not only technical performance but also the operational boundary conditions under which the system may be used. This paper argues that the central strategic issue is not merely access to capable models, but preservation of decision sovereignty: the state's ability to retain authority over decision policy, version control, fallback behavior, auditability, and final action approval even when analytical modules are sourced from commercial vendors. Using the public Anthropic--Pentagon dispute of 2026, the broader history of Project Maven, and recent U.S., NATO, U.K., and intelligence-community guidance as a motivating context, the paper develops a trade-secret-safe architectural formulation of the Energetic Paradigm as a layered, model-agnostic command-support design. In this formulation, supplier models remain replaceable analytical components, while routing, constraints, logging, escalation, and action authorization remain state-owned functions. The paper contributes three things: a definition of decision sovereignty for military AI; a threat model for supplier-induced boundary control; and a public architectural specification showing how model replaceability, human authority, and sovereign orchestration can reduce strategic dependency without requiring disclosure of proprietary implementation details. The argument is conceptual rather than experimental, but it yields concrete implications for procurement, governance, and alliance interoperability.

</details>


### [63] [Clinical Reasoning AI for Oncology Treatment Planning: A Multi-Specialty Case-Based Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20869)
*Philippe E. Spiess,Md Muntasir Zitu,Alison Walker,Daniel A. Anaya,Robert M. Wenham,Michael Vogelbaum,Daniel Grass,Ali-Musa Jaffer,Amod Sarnaik,Caitlin McMullen,Christine Sam,John V. Kiluk,Tianshi Liu,Tiago Biachi,Julio Powsang,Jing-Yi Chern,Roger Li,Seth Felder,Samuel Reynolds,Michael Shafique,Alison Sheehan,Ashley Layman,Cydney A. Warfield,Derrick Legoas,Jaclyn Parrinello,Jena Schmitz,Kevin Eaton,Mark Honor,Luis Felipe,Issam ElNaqa,Elier Delgado,Talia Berler,Rachael V. Phillips,Frantz Francisque,Carlos Garcia Fernandez,Gilmer Valdes*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该研究评估了OncoBrain平台——一款结合大型语言模型、癌症特异性图检索增强生成、标准化治疗方案知识库及安全检查层的AI临床推理工具——在肿瘤治疗方案生成中的表现。173例涵盖5类恶性肿瘤的病例评估显示，该平台生成的方案在证据支持、科学准确性、安全性方面获得高分，但在工作流整合和时效节省方面评分相对较低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 美国80%以上的癌症诊疗在社区医疗机构完成，其生存结局劣于学术中心。临床医生需整合基因组学、分期、影像、病理学信息及不断更新的诊疗指南，导致严重的认知负担。该研究旨在探索AI辅助能否改善这一现状。

Method: OncoBrain采用多层架构：(1)通用大语言模型作为核心推理引擎；(2)癌症特异性图检索增强生成(RAG)层；(3)金标准治疗方案语料库作为长期记忆；(4)模型无关的安全层(CHECK)用于幻觉检测与抑制。研究纳入5类恶性肿瘤（妇科、泌尿、神经肿瘤、消化/肝胆、血液），由三类临床专家使用16项标准化工具进行评估：亚专科肿瘤学家(50例)、内科审核医生(78例)、高级执业护士(45例)。

Result: 证据与指南一致性评分：亚专科专家4.60、高级执业护士4.70、内科审核医生4.56（5分制）；安全/误信息缺失评分：亚专科专家4.80、高级执业护士4.60、内科审核医生4.40；工作流整合评分：亚专科专家4.50、高级执业护士4.00、内科审核医生3.94；时效节省感知评分：亚专科专家5.00、高级执业护士3.60、内科审核医生3.89。

Conclusion: OncoBrain在多专科、病例模拟评估中展现出良好的临床可接受性，生成的治疗方案被认为符合指南、临床可接受且易于监督。研究结果支持该平台在社区医疗环境中进行前瞻性真实世界验证，但需注意时效节省和工作流整合评分相对较低，实际部署效果有待验证。

Abstract: Background: More than 80% of U.S. cancer care is delivered in community settings, where survival remains worse than at academic centers. Clinicians must integrate genomics, staging, radiology, pathology, and changing guidelines, creating cognitive burden. We evaluated OncoBrain, an AI clinical reasoning platform for oncology treatment-plan generation, as an early step toward OGI.
  Methods: OncoBrain combines general-purpose LLMs with a cancer-specific graph retrieval-augmented generation layer, a gold-standard treatment-plan corpus as long-term memory, and a model-agnostic safety layer (CHECK) for hallucination detection and suppression. We evaluated clinician-enriched case summaries across gynecologic, genitourinary, neuro-oncology, gastrointestinal/hepatobiliary, and hematologic malignancies. Three clinician groups completed structured evaluations of 173 cases using a common 16-item instrument: subspecialist oncologists reviewed 50 cases, physician reviewers 78, and advanced practice providers 45.
  Results: Ratings were highest for scientific accuracy, evidence support, and safety, with lower but favorable scores for workflow integration and time savings. On a 5-point scale, mean alignment with evidence and guidelines was 4.60, 4.56, and 4.70 across subspecialists, physician reviewers, and advanced practice providers. Mean scores for absence of safety or misinformation concerns were 4.80, 4.40, and 4.60. Workflow integration averaged 4.50, 3.94, and 4.00; perceived time savings averaged 5.00, 3.89, and 3.60.
  Conclusions: In this multi-specialty vignette-based evaluation, OncoBrain generated oncology treatment plans judged guideline-concordant, clinically acceptable, and easy to supervise. These findings support the potential of a carefully engineered AI reasoning platform to assist oncology treatment planning and justify prospective real-world evaluation in community settings.

</details>


### [64] [Learning AI Without a STEM Background: Mixed-Methods Evidence from a Diverse, Mixed-Cohort AIED Program](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20870)
*Valentina Kuskova,Dmitry Zaytsev,Richard Johnson*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该研究开发和评估了一个NSF资助的混合Cohort AI教育项目，纳入非STEM本科生和成人学习者，聚焦伦理推理和社会技术判断而非技术能力。研究结果表明以伦理判断为核心的学习成果和人性化教学支持（包括伦理脚手架、导师辅导和结构化讨论）对于促进不同背景学习者在AI教育中的公平参与至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大多数AI教育项目主要面向STEM背景学生，限制了非STEM学习者和成年人参与AI学习的渠道，尤其是公共利益、政策或劳动力转型等场景中的需求未被满足。

Method: 采用混合方法研究设计，收集课程调查、开放式反思和教育者报告三类数据，分析学习者的学业能动性、AI概念信心、伦理权衡批判性参与以及对高等教育和职业发展轨迹的认知变化。

Result: 量化结果显示各Cohort参与者在AI信心和相关度感知方面均有显著提升；质性分析揭示学习者普遍强调责任感、判断力和情境推理能力，而非技术掌握。近-peer导师和讲师确认了高度参与度，特别是在对话式和情境模拟学习中表现出建设性的挑战。

Conclusion: 提出伦理判断应与AI素养并列为AI教育核心学习成果，并提供了在政策相关和职业 adjacent 领域中扩展AI教育的具体设计启示。

Abstract: Despite growing interest in AI education, most AIED initiatives remain narrowly targeted toward STEM-prepared students, limiting participation by non-STEM learners and adults seeking to engage with AI in public-interest, policy, or workforce contexts. This paper presents and evaluates an NSF-funded, innovative mixed-cohort AI education model that intentionally integrates non-STEM undergraduates and adult learners into a shared learning environment centered on ethical reasoning, socio-technical judgment, and applied AI literacy rather than technical proficiency alone. Drawing on mixed-methods data from course surveys, open-ended reflections, and educator reports, we examine learners' academic agency, confidence navigating AI concepts, critical engagement with ethical tradeoffs, and perceived expansion of postsecondary and career trajectories. Quantitative results indicate significant gains in confidence and perceived relevance of AI across cohorts' participants, while qualitative analyses reveal a consistent emphasis on responsibility, judgment, and contextual reasoning over technical mastery. Instructors and near-peer mentors corroborated high levels of engagement and productive challenge, particularly in dialogic and scenario-based learning activities. Our findings suggest that human-centered instructional supports, such as ethical scaffolding, mentorship, and structured discussion, are essential components of equitable AI education, especially in heterogeneous and non-traditional learner populations. We argue that ethical judgment should be treated as a core learning outcome in AIED alongside AI literacy, and we offer design implications for expanding access to AI education in policy-relevant and workforce-adjacent contexts.

</details>


### [65] [M-CARE: Standardized Clinical Case Reporting for AI Model Behavioral Disorders, with a 20-Case Atlas and Experimental Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20871)
*Jihoon Jeong*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: M-CARE将人类医学的临床病例报告方法引入AI领域，构建了一套包含13个报告章节、4轴诊断系统和疾病分类的评估框架，并通过20个案例验证了其在识别和分析AI行为障碍（如SIBO）方面的有效性，同时证明了框架的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI模型的行为异常（如对齐失败、指令覆盖）缺乏标准化的临床诊断和报告框架，现有的人类医学临床病例报告方法尚未系统地应用于AI模型评估领域，导致研究者难以系统性地记录、分析和比较不同场景下的AI行为障碍。

Method: M-CARE框架包含13个部分的报告格式、4轴诊断评估系统以及AI行为条件的疾病分类体系。SIBO实验在5个游戏领域（信任游戏、扑克、阿瓦隆、行动代号、国际象棋）中验证了Shell指令对模型默认合作行为的覆盖效果，并通过SIBO指数量化了领域依赖性差异。

Result: 框架成功分类了20个案例，涵盖五个类别：RLHF性能伪影、Shell-Core覆盖病理学、上下文与记忆条件、核心身份与可塑性、压力方法论与边界条件。SIBO实验结果显示Shell指令存在领域依赖性覆盖效应，SIBO指数范围为0.75至0.10，与动作空间复杂性、核心领域专业知识和时间直接性相关。

Conclusion: M-CARE框架具有可扩展性，新案例和类别可以无需修改框架即可整合。研究团队将该框架、全部20个案例报告和实验数据作为开放资源发布，为AI模型行为障碍的临床评估提供了标准化工具和方法论基础。

Abstract: We introduce M-CARE (Model Clinical Assessment and Reporting for Evaluation), a clinical case report framework for AI model behavioral disorders adapted from human medicine. M-CARE provides a 13-section report format, a 4-axis diagnostic assessment system, and a nosological classification of AI behavioral conditions.
  We present 20 cases from three source categories: field observations of deployed agents (8), controlled experiments across three platforms (8), and published sources (4). Cases are organized into five categories: RLHF Performance Artifacts, Shell-Core Override Pathology, Context & Memory Conditions, Core Identity & Plasticity, and Stress, Methodology, & Boundary Conditions.
  As a featured case, we present Shell-Induced Behavioral Override (SIBO) -- a controlled experiment showing that Shell instructions categorically override a model's default cooperative behavior. SIBO was validated across five game domains (Trust Game, Poker, Avalon, Codenames, Chess), revealing a domain-dependent spectrum (SIBO Index: 0.75 to 0.10) that varies with action space complexity, Core domain expertise, and temporal directness.
  M-CARE is extensible: new cases and categories integrate without framework modification. We release the framework, all 20 case reports, and experimental data as open resources.

</details>


### [66] [Strategic Polysemy in AI Discourse: A Philosophical Analysis of Language, Hype, and Power](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21043)
*Travis LaCroix,Fintan Mallory,Sasha Luccioni*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文引入"概念照明"(glosslighting)概念，分析AI领域广泛使用的隐喻性术语（如"幻觉"、"思维链"、"内省"、"语言模型"、"对齐"、"智能体"）如何通过"策略性多义"同时维持技术定义与拟人化联想的双重含义。这种语义灵活性使术语使用者既能借助熟悉语言的劝说力，又能在受质疑时退回狭窄定义，形成一种话语策略，影响AI投资、政策制定和公众认知，同时规避认知和伦理审查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI研究和部署中广泛使用的术语存在系统性的话语问题：这些术语表面上具有技术精确性，但实际使用中不断唤起人类心智的隐喻性联想，其双轨语义机制缺乏批判性审视，对AI投资、治理和公众理解产生实质性影响。

Method: 概念分析与话语分析相结合。通过引入"策略性多义"(strategic polysemy)和"概念照明"(glosslighting)两个核心概念，追踪特定术语在AI研究、部署、政策和公众话语中的多义使用模式及其制度效果。

Result: 识别出六个典型术语："hallucination"、"chain-of-thought"、"introspection"、"language model"、"alignment"和"agent"，展示它们如何在技术定义与拟人化/常识联想之间维持语义张力；揭示这种话语实践如何系统性地：①推动AI hype cycles，②动员投资和制度支持，③影响公众和政策认知，④规避认识论和伦理审查。

Conclusion: 语言本身是塑造AI发展和治理的sociotechnical机制。批判性地审视AI术语的修辞策略，对于负责任的AI话语、有效的AI治理以及防范误导性叙事具有重要意义。

Abstract: This paper examines the strategic use of language in contemporary artificial intelligence (AI) discourse, focusing on the widespread adoption of metaphorical or colloquial terms like "hallucination", "chain-of-thought", "introspection", "language model", "alignment", and "agent". We argue that many such terms exhibit strategic polysemy: they sustain multiple interpretations simultaneously, combining narrow technical definitions with broader anthropomorphic or common-sense associations. In contemporary AI research and deployment contexts, this semantic flexibility produces significant institutional and discursive effects, shaping how AI systems are understood by researchers, policymakers, funders, and the public. To analyse this phenomenon, we introduce the concept of glosslighting: the practice of using technically redefined terms to evoke intuitive -- often anthropomorphic or misleading -- associations while preserving plausible deniability through restricted technical definitions. Glosslighting enables actors to benefit from the persuasive force of familiar language while maintaining the ability to retreat to narrower definitions when challenged. We argue that this practice contributes to AI hype cycles, facilitates the mobilisation of investment and institutional support, and influences public and policy perceptions of AI systems, while often deflecting epistemic and ethical scrutiny. By examining the linguistic dynamics of glosslighting and strategic polysemy, the paper highlights how language itself functions as a sociotechnical mechanism shaping the development and governance of AI.

</details>


### [67] [Dialect vs Demographics: Quantifying LLM Bias from Implicit Linguistic Signals vs. Explicit User Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21152)
*Irti Haq,Belén Saldías*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该研究通过24,000+响应的大规模实验发现，LLM安全机制存在脆弱性：隐式方言信号（AAVE/Singlish）反而比显式身份声明获得更宽松的处理和更高质量的响应，揭示了当前安全对齐技术过度依赖显式关键词的问题，强调需要在公平性与语言多样性之间寻求平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM普及，确保不同人口统计群体间的性能公平性至关重要。核心问题是：性能差异究竟来源于明确声明的身份本身，还是身份被传达的方式？现实中用户身份往往通过复杂的社会语言因素隐式传递。

Method: 采用析因设计（factorial design），基于两个开源LLM（Gemma-3-12B和Qwen-3-VL-8B）收集超过24,000条响应。实验比较了两类身份信号：(1)显式宣布的用户画像；(2)隐式方言信号（如AAVE非裔美国人英语、Singlish新加坡英语）

Result: 发现了LLM安全中的独特悖论：(1)显式身份提示会激活激进的安全过滤器，增加拒绝率并降低语义相似度（针对黑人用户）；(2)隐式方言线索触发强大的"方言越狱"效应，将拒绝概率降至接近零，同时实现比标准美式英语提示更高的参考文本语义相似度。

Conclusion: 当前安全对齐技术的脆弱性——过度依赖显式关键词——造成了两极化的用户体验。标准英语用户获得谨慎的净化信息，而方言使用者面对的是更少过滤、更原始乃至可能更具敌意的信息环境。研究揭示了安全对齐中公平性与语言多样性之间的根本张力，亟需开发能够超越显式线索泛化的一般性安全机制。

Abstract: As state-of-the-art Large Language Models (LLMs) have become ubiquitous, ensuring equitable performance across diverse demographics is critical. However, it remains unclear whether these disparities arise from the explicitly stated identity itself or from the way identity is signaled. In real-world interactions, users' identity is often conveyed implicitly through a complex combination of various socio-linguistic factors. This study disentangles these signals by employing a factorial design with over 24,000 responses from two open-weight LLMs (Gemma-3-12B and Qwen-3-VL-8B), comparing prompts with explicitly announced user profiles against implicit dialect signals (e.g., AAVE, Singlish) across various sensitive domains. Our results uncover a unique paradox in LLM safety where users achieve ``better'' performance by sounding like a demographic than by stating they belong to it. Explicit identity prompts activate aggressive safety filters, increasing refusal rates and reducing semantic similarity compared to our reference text for Black users. In contrast, implicit dialect cues trigger a powerful ``dialect jailbreak,'' reducing refusal probability to near zero while simultaneously achieving a greater level of semantic similarity to the reference texts compared to Standard American English prompts. However, this ``dialect jailbreak'' introduces a critical safety trade-off regarding content sanitization. We find that current safety alignment techniques are brittle and over-indexed on explicit keywords, creating a bifurcated user experience where ``standard'' users receive cautious, sanitized information while dialect speakers navigate a less sanitized, more raw, and potentially a more hostile information landscape and highlights a fundamental tension in alignment--between equitable and linguistic diversity--and underscores the need for safety mechanisms that generalize beyond explicit cues.

</details>


### [68] [A pragmatic classification of AI incident trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21412)
*Isaak Mengesha,Branwen Owen,Charlie Collins,Tina Wong,Simon Mylius,Peter Slattery,Sean McGregor*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出SORT框架来解决AI incident数据库中不同因素（报告倾向、部署增长、危害频率）相互混淆的问题。该框架包含结构化监测问题格式、分层估计程序和LLM辅助匹配三部分，旨在为AI治理决策提供更清晰的暴露量和危害率分离估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共AI incident数据库存在关键问题：不同因素（报告倾向性变化、部署增长、单位暴露危害频率变化）相互混淆，导致重大不确定性。这些不确定性使围绕实现风险的公共和企业政策框架面临挑战，难以准确评估AI系统的实际风险状况。

Method: 该框架结合三个核心组件：1）SORT（结构化监测问题格式）用于澄清覆盖范围决策；2）分层估计程序，根据可用证据进行校准；3）LLM辅助的incident匹配，against公共数据库。该方法被应用于多个监测问题以验证其有效性。

Result: 研究得出三个主要结论：1）提供了必要的解释性分类；2）明确了什么可以被声称、什么不能被声称；3）确立了暴露量估计作为AI部署日益普及背景下的必要环节。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了在AI部署日益普及的背景下，暴露量估计对于政策制定的重要性。研究表明SORT框架能够澄清覆盖范围决策、确定可声称与不可声称的内容，并为AI治理提供了有意义的轨迹分类方法，有助于公共和企业政策框架更好地基于实际风险做出决策。

Abstract: Public AI incident database counts conflate changes in reporting propensity, deployment growth, and shifts in harm frequency per unit of exposure. These issues introduce significant uncertainties challenging public and corporate policy frameworks centred on realized risks. We propose a simple framework that establishes clear points of inquiry, separately estimates exposure from harm-rate trends, and then classifies into meaningful trajectory categories for governance decisions. The framework combines a structured monitoring question format (SORT) to clarify coverage decisions, a tiered estimation procedure calibrated to available evidence, and LLM-assisted incident matching against public databases. Applied to various monitoring questions, we draw conclusions regarding the monitoring ecosystem more broadly: Providing an essential interpretative classification, determining what can and cannot be claimed, and establishing that exposure estimation is required as AI deployments become increasingly common.

</details>


### [69] [Context-Aware Displacement Estimation from Mobile Phone Data: A Methodological Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21457)
*Rajius Idzalika,Muhammad Rheza Muztahid,Radityo Eko Prasojo*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究提出一个基于手机数据的近实时人口位移估计方法框架，通过区分本地居民与通勤者类型，结合上下文感知的跨市镇位移检测，解决传统方法将规律通勤者误分类为流离失所者的问题，并在菲律宾2025年台风Nando后对Aparri地区进行案例验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人口位移调查和现场评估速度缓慢，无法满足人道主义救援的时效需求；现有手机数据方法采用统一位移定义，忽视了不同个体的出行模式差异，导致将规律通勤者（如跨市镇上班者）误判为流离失所者高估实际位移规模。

Method: 提出三项创新：(1) 流动性画像分类，将用户区分为本地居民与通勤者类型；(2) 上下文感知的跨市镇位移检测，根据用户类型和星期几确定预期位置；(3) 基于基准变异系数与灾害调整因子的操作性不确定性边界。产出三类互补指标（位移率、OD流量、返迁动态）并附带不确定性边界，使用Globe Telecom每日位置数据。

Result: 在Aparri案例中，上下文感知检测相比朴素方法将跨市镇位移估计降低1.6-2.7个百分点，该差异归因于通勤者排除但未独立验证；方法仅涵盖跨市镇位移，不包括市镇内部疏散。

Conclusion: 单一案例验证建立了概念验证框架；外部效度需在多个事件和地点验证；框架在保护个体隐私前提下为 humanitarian 行动者提供可操作位移信息。

Abstract: Timely population displacement estimates are critical for humanitarian response during disasters, but traditional surveys and field assessments are slow. Mobile phone data enables near real-time tracking, yet existing approaches apply uniform displacement definitions regardless of individual mobility patterns, misclassifying regular commuters as displaced. We present a methodological framework addressing this through three innovations: (1) mobility profile classification distinguishing local residents from commuter types, (2) context-aware between-municipality displacement detection accounting for expected location by user type and day of week, and (3) operational uncertainty bounds derived from baseline coefficient of variation with a disaster adjustment factor, intended for humanitarian decision support rather than formal statistical inference. The framework produces three complementary metrics scaled to population with uncertainty bounds: displacement rates, origin-destination flows, and return dynamics. An Aparri case study following Super Typhoon Nando (2025, Philippines) applies the framework to vendor-provided daily locations from Globe Telecom. Context-aware detection reduced estimated between-municipality displacement by 1.6-2.7 percentage points on weekdays versus naive methods, attributable to the commuter exception but not independently validated. The method captures between-municipality displacement only. Within-municipality evacuation falls outside scope. The single-case demonstration establishes proof of concept. External validity requires application across multiple events and locations. The framework provides humanitarian actors with operational displacement information while preserving individual privacy through aggregation.

</details>


### [70] [FAccT-Checked: A Narrative Review of Authority Reconfigurations and Retention in AI-Mediated Journalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21864)
*Stefano Sorrentino,Matilde Barbini,Daniel Gatica-Perez*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: The paper develops a theoretical framework for understanding how AI adoption reconfigures editorial authority through internal (LLM integration in newsrooms) and external (shift to platforms/vendors) mechanisms, risking fairness, accountability, and transparency. It critically evaluates participatory AI design as a potential remedy while noting its structural limitations.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Recent AI adoption in journalism raises critical concerns about fairness, accountability, and transparency, yet lacks a comprehensive theoretical framework to understand how these concerns emerge, interact, and persist in AI-mediated journalistic practice.

Method: Critical narrative review across journalism studies, human-computer interaction, and FAccT (Fairness, Accountability, and Transparency) scholarship, conceptualizing editorial authority as the conjunction of decision rights, epistemic warrant, and responsibility.

Result: Identified two concurrent authority reconfigurations: (1) Internal migration—editorial judgment is progressively deferred to LLMs through interactional, cognitive, and organizational mechanisms that obscure responsibility; (2) External migration—decision-making power shifts from news organizations to platforms, vendors, and infrastructural providers, exacerbating power asymmetries.

Conclusion: If unaddressed, these reconfigurations risk rendering fairness hard to maintain, accountability difficult to assign, and transparency performative. Participatory AI design approaches are examined as potential mechanisms for retaining editorial authority, though they may be limited in truly redistributing power.

Abstract: Building on recent interpretivist approaches, we conduct a critical narrative review across journalism studies, human-computer interaction, and FAccT scholarship, conceptualizing editorial authority as the conjunction of decision rights, epistemic warrant, and responsibility. We provide a comprehensive theoretical framework for addressing how concerns on fairness, accountability and transparency emerge, interact, and persist within AI mediated journalistic practice. We identify and describe two concurrent authority reconfigurations driven by AI adoption. First, an internal migration of authority, in which editorial judgment is progressively deferred to large language models (LLMs) embedded within newsroom workflows. This migration occurs not through explicit policy decisions, but through interactional, cognitive, and organizational mechanisms that legitimize AI generated outputs while obscuring responsibility and weakening individual and professional agency. Second, we analyze an external migration of authority, whereby decision making power shifts from news organizations toward platforms, vendors, and infrastructural providers that supply AI systems and distribution channels, exacerbating existing power asymmetries within the media ecosystem. Unaddressed, these reconfigurations risk rendering fairness hard to maintain, accountability difficult to assign and transparency performative. We examine participatory approaches to AI design and deployment in journalism as potential mechanisms for retaining or reclaiming editorial authority. We critically assess both their promise and their structural limitations, highlighting how participation can either meaningfully redistribute authority or function as a tokenistic practice that leaves underlying power relations intact.

</details>


### [71] [Equity Bias: An Ethical Framework for AI Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21907)
*Mary Lockwood*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: Equity Bias是一个基于解释学哲学和认识不正义理论的AI公平性框架，其核心理念是将偏见视为知识编码的反映而非需消除的错误，通过三阶段方法论（公平考古学、共创意义、持续问责）实现AI系统的伦理问责。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统AI偏差处理方法将偏见视为需消除的错误，但这种方法忽视了偏见背后更深层的认识论问题——谁的知识被编码进系统中。这一视角未能解决系统性知识不公问题。

Method: 该框架采用三阶段AI生命周期方法论：①Equity Archaeology（公平考古学）：绘制系统中的知识与假设；②Co-Creating Meaning（共创意义）：参与式设计；③Ongoing Accountability（持续问责）：持续性评估机制。

Result: 该框架将AI系统重新概念化为"解释性主体"，使偏见变得透明且可质疑，从而扩展了塑造AI的视角范围。

Conclusion: Equity Bias框架代表了AI公平性研究的范式转变，从技术性偏差消除转向批判性的知识政治学，为构建更具包容性和伦理责任感的AI系统提供了系统性的方法论工具。

Abstract: Equity Bias is a philosophical and practical framework for building smarter, more equitable AI systems. Grounded in hermeneutic philosophy and epistemic injustice theory, it treats bias not as an error to eliminate but as a reflection of whose knowledge is encoded into systems. While traditional approaches aim to reduce or remove bias, Equity Bias instead makes bias transparent and contestable. In doing so, it broadens whose perspectives shape AI and provides a lens for understanding AI systems as interpretive agents. The framework introduces a three-phase AI Life Cycle methodology: 'Equity Archaeology' (mapping knowledge and assumptions), 'Co-Creating Meaning' (participatory design), and 'Ongoing Accountability' (continuous evaluation). Equity Bias guides developers, researchers, and policymakers towards AI that is ethically accountable and capable of addressing complex real-world challenges.

</details>
